===== FILE backend/tests/test_redline_short_circuit.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import pathlib

from app.workflow_model_executor import (
    REDLINE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MARKER,
    redline_short_circuit_output,
)

_POLICY = json.loads(
    (pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures_redline_policy_v101.json").read_text()
)


def _snapshot(policy: dict | None = None, category: str = "inspiration_image") -> dict:
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "eligibility_redline_1",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "config": {
                "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "content_json": json.dumps(policy if policy is not None else _POLICY)
                },
            },
        }
    ]
    return {
        "workflow": {"manifest": {"category_key": category, "nodes": nodes}}
    }


def _node(asset_key: str = "prompt_b") -> dict:
    return {"kind": "prompt", "config": {"asset_key": asset_key}}


def _precheck(*, screenshot: bool) -> dict:
    return {
        "redline_triggered": {
            "screenshot": screenshot,
            "casual_photo": False,
            "text_heavy": False,
            "qr_code_heavy": False,
            "inverted_image": False,
        },
        "reason": ["是截图"] if screenshot else [],
        "production_fields": {"title": "测试", "reason": "是截图" if screenshot else ""},
        "hard_defects": [],
        "image_defects": [],
        "decisive_evidence": {
            "redline_triggered": {
                "screenshot": ["页面顶部有浏览器地址栏"] if screenshot else [],
                "casual_photo": [],
                "text_heavy": [],
                "qr_code_heavy": [],
                "inverted_image": [],
            },
            "hard_defects": [],
            "image_defects": [],
        },
        "decision_status": "complete",
        "uncertain_fields": [],
        "track_classification": "class_one",
        "media_type": "real_photo",
    }


# 注意：真实流水线里 B 的 upstream 只有直接前驱（赛道路由），拿不到 A 的
# 输出——首版测试用 A 直连的假拓扑，全绿但线上零触发（run138）。判定函数
# 因此改为显式接收调用方从运行步骤记录取出的 precheck。


def test_redline_hit_short_circuits_prompt_b() -> None:
    """调用 A 判红线后必须跳过调用 B——run137 里 28/250 条红线素材白花了 B 的钱。"""

    output = redline_short_circuit_output(
        _snapshot(), _node(), _precheck(screenshot=True)
    )
    assert output is not None
    assert output[REDLINE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MARKER] is True
    assert "screenshot" in output["hit_rules"]
    assert output["short_circuit_decision"]["target_level"] == "L5"
    assert output["short_circuit_decision"]["rule_key"] == "legacy-redline-l5"


def test_no_redline_means_no_short_circuit() -> None:
    assert (
        redline_short_circuit_output(
            _snapshot(), _node(), _precheck(screenshot=False)
        )
        is None
    )


def test_short_circuit_only_applies_to_prompt_b() -> None:
    assert (
        redline_short_circuit_output(
            _snapshot(),
            _node("prompt_a"),
            _precheck(screenshot=True),
        )
        is None
    )


def test_disabled_policy_never_short_circuits() -> None:
    policy = dict(_POLICY)
    policy["enabled"] = False
    assert (
        redline_short_circuit_output(
            _snapshot(policy),
            _node(),
            _precheck(screenshot=True),
        )
        is None
    )


def test_missing_precheck_falls_back_to_calling_b() -> None:
    assert redline_short_circuit_output(_snapshot(), _node(), None) is None


def test_broken_precheck_falls_back_to_calling_b() -> None:
    """判不动就照常调 B：短路是省钱优化，不能变成新的失败面。"""

    assert (
        redline_short_circuit_output(_snapshot(), _node(), {"junk": 1})
        is None
    )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_regression_online_mode.py =====
"""存量回归「使用在线推理」开关（合同 2026-09-13-regression-online-mode）。

开关默认关闭＝本批批量；打开＝本批在线。模式在创建时冻结，重试与恢复沿用冻结值，
两个 run 之间不共享任何进程级开关。必要配置缺失在发起付费调用之前受控拒绝。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import model_pricing
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import ModelRegistryEntry, ProductionRun
from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

BATCH_ENDPOINT = "ep-bi-20260909104719-bkhmk"
ONLINE_ENDPOINT = "ep-20260901120000-abcde"
BATCH_MODEL = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"


def _settings(db: Session, **fields):
    from app.doubao_endpoint_models import VolcengineBillingSettings

    row = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1) or VolcengineBillingSettings(id=1)
    for key, value in fields.items():
        setattr(row, key, value)
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _stock_fixture(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    *,
    name: str = "regression-online-mode",
    model_id: str = BATCH_MODEL,
    endpoint_model: str | None = None,
    bind_online: bool = True,
    bind_batch: bool = True,
    routing_enabled: bool = True,
):
    """一个真实的存量回归夹具：豆包 2.0 计价模型 + 可选的在线/批量接入点绑定。"""

    from app.doubao_endpoints import bind_endpoint, create_endpoint
    from app.models import EvaluationCategoryProfile
    from test_native_baseline_regression import _baseline
    from test_native_workflow_real_regression import (
        _attach_priced_model,
        _available_test_model_secret,
    )
    from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism

    _available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'{name}.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine)
    db.add(EvaluationCategoryProfile(category_key="space_image", display_name="空间图"))
    db.flush()
    baseline, workflow = _baseline(db), _mechanism(db)
    model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
    model.model_id = model_id
    model.pricing_contract_json = canonical_json(
        model_pricing.doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    )
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    snap = prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]
    snap["model_id"] = model.model_id
    snap["runtime_config"] = build_model_config_snapshot(model)
    snap.pop("snapshot_hash", None)
    snap["snapshot_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(snap).encode()).hexdigest()
    registry_id = prompt["config"]["model_registry_id"]
    db.add(
        ModelRegistryEntry(
            id=registry_id,
            name="接入点路由登记",
            provider="doubao",
            model_id=model.model_id,
            base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3",
            api_path="/chat/completions",
        )
    )
    workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
    workflow.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)).encode()
    ).hexdigest()
    bindings = []
    if bind_online:
        bindings.append(ONLINE_ENDPOINT)
    if bind_batch:
        bindings.append(BATCH_ENDPOINT)
    for endpoint_id in bindings:
        row = create_endpoint(
            db,
            endpoint_id=endpoint_id,
            display_name=endpoint_id,
            category_key="space_image",
            foundation_model=endpoint_model or model.model_id,
            actor="test",
        )
        bind_endpoint(
            db,
            row.id,
            workflow_definition_id=workflow.workflow_definition_id,
            expected_revision=1,
            reason="test",
            actor="test",
        )
    _settings(db, endpoint_routing_enabled=routing_enabled)
    db.flush()
    return engine, db, baseline, workflow


def _second_baseline(db: Session):
    """第二个基准集要有自己的素材，才能证明两个 run 各自持有路由。"""

    from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem

    asset = Asset(
        original_name="native-stock-2.jpg",
        stored_name="native-stock-2.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=20,
        sha256="e" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="space_image",
        name="并发对照基准集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="b" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level="L2",
            expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
            truth_contract_key="level.v1",
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return baseline


def _create(db, baseline, workflow, **kwargs):
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    payload = {
        "actor": "test",
        "model_execution_mode": "real",
        "real_execution_confirmed": True,
    }
    payload.update(kwargs)
    payload.setdefault("idempotency_key", f"key-{payload.get('inference_mode', 'x')}")
    return create_native_baseline_run(
        db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, **payload
    )


def _frozen_modes(db, run) -> tuple[str, str, set[str]]:
    execution = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)
    runtime_modes = {
        json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]["inference_route"][
            "mode"
        ]
        for production in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all()
        if json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"].get("baseline_run_id")
        == run.id
    }
    return (
        execution["inference_mode"],
        execution["inference_route"]["mode"],
        runtime_modes,
    )

    import inspect

    assert BaselineRunCreateRequest().inference_mode == "online"
    assert (
        inspect.signature(preview_baseline_real_execution)
        .parameters["inference_mode"]
        .default
        == "online"
    )


def test_explicit_batch_run_freezes_batch_everywhere(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        from app.native_baseline_regression import preview_real_execution_budget

        preview = preview_real_execution_budget(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            item_count=1,
            inference_mode="batch",
        )
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch")
        mode, route_mode, runtime_modes = _frozen_modes(db, run)
        assert mode == "batch" and route_mode == "batch"
        assert runtime_modes == {"batch"}
        execution = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)
        assert execution["inference_route"]["endpoint_id"] == BATCH_ENDPOINT
        assert preview["inference_route"] == execution["inference_route"]
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C2 同源选路与计价
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_preview_and_start_price_the_same_route(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """预估与建 run 是两次独立请求，稳定配置下必须解析出同一条路由与同一份价目。"""

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        from app.native_baseline_regression import preview_real_execution_budget

        batch_preview = preview_real_execution_budget(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            item_count=1,
            inference_mode="batch",
        )
        online_preview = preview_real_execution_budget(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            item_count=1,
            inference_mode="online",
        )
        assert batch_preview["inference_route"]["endpoint_id"] == BATCH_ENDPOINT
        assert online_preview["inference_route"]["endpoint_id"] == ONLINE_ENDPOINT
        # 批量价目是在线的一半（每档输入 300 对 600、输出 1800 对 3600）。
        # 逐节点取整会带来至多每节点 1 微元的差，这里独立手算上下界，不复述被测代码。
        batch_cost = batch_preview["expected"]["cost_micros"]
        online_cost = online_preview["expected"]["cost_micros"]
        node_count = len(online_preview["node_pricing"])
        assert abs(online_cost - batch_cost * 2) <= 2 * node_count
        assert [
            (tier["input_micros_per_1k"], tier["output_micros_per_1k"])
            for tier in batch_preview["node_pricing"][0]["pricing_tiers"]
        ] == [(300, 1800), (450, 2700), (900, 5400)]
        assert [
            (tier["input_micros_per_1k"], tier["output_micros_per_1k"])
            for tier in online_preview["node_pricing"][0]["pricing_tiers"]
        ] == [(600, 3600), (900, 5400), (1800, 10800)]
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="online")
        execution = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)
        assert execution["inference_route"] == online_preview["inference_route"]
        limits = json.loads(
            db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_json
        )["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"]
        tier = next(iter(limits.values()))["pricing_snapshot"]["tiers"][0]
        assert tier["input_micros_per_1k"] == 600
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_batch_start_prices_with_the_batch_contract(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch")
        limits = json.loads(
            db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_json
        )["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"]
        tier = next(iter(limits.values()))["pricing_snapshot"]["tiers"][0]
        assert tier["input_micros_per_1k"] == 300
        assert run.status != "failed"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

def test_concurrent_batch_and_online_runs_keep_their_own_routes(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        second = _second_baseline(db)
        batch_run = _create(
            db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch", idempotency_key="k-batch"
        )
        online_run = _create(
            db, second, workflow, inference_mode="online", idempotency_key="k-online"
        )
        assert _frozen_modes(db, batch_run)[2] == {"batch"}
        assert _frozen_modes(db, online_run)[2] == {"online"}
        assert _frozen_modes(db, batch_run)[0] == "batch"
        assert _frozen_modes(db, online_run)[0] == "online"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_rebinding_endpoints_does_not_move_a_frozen_run(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """重试/恢复读的是 run 自己的冻结快照，之后改绑不影响本轮。"""

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        from types import SimpleNamespace

        from app.doubao_endpoints import resolve_run_endpoint
        from app.models import WorkflowDefinition

        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch")
        production = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        route = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"][
            "inference_route"
        ]
        definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, workflow.workflow_definition_id)
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = None
        definition.doubao_inference_mode = "online"
        db.flush()
        assert (
            resolve_run_endpoint(
                db,
                SimpleNamespace(doubao_endpoint_id=None),
                provider="doubao",
                model_id=BATCH_MODEL,
                inference_route=route,
            )
            == BATCH_ENDPOINT
        )
        assert json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)["inference_mode"] == "batch"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

def test_online_without_an_active_endpoint_is_rejected_before_any_run_exists(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        NativeBaselineRegressionError,
        preview_real_execution_budget,
    )
    from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch, bind_online=False
    )
    try:
        with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as preview_error:
            preview_real_execution_budget(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                item_count=1,
                inference_mode="online",
            )
        assert preview_error.value.code == "doubao_online_endpoint_missing"
        assert preview_error.value.status_code == 422
        with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as create_error:
            _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="online")
        assert create_error.value.code == "doubao_online_endpoint_missing"
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionRun)).all() == []
        assert db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all() == []
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 本批批量就是本批批量：显式/默认 batch 不得被冻结在线兜底
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _fallback_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch, **kwargs):
    """全局在线兜底开关打开，且同工作流绑定了已验收、同底层模型的在线接入点。"""

    from app.doubao_endpoints import DoubaoInferenceEndpoint

    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK", "true")
    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _stock_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch, **kwargs)
    online = db.scalar(
        select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint).where(
            DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.endpoint_id == ONLINE_ENDPOINT
        )
    )
    if online is not None:
        online.verification_status = "verified"
        db.flush()
    return engine, db, baseline, workflow


def _runtime_routes(db, run):
    return [
        json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]["inference_route"]
        for production in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all()
        if json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"].get("baseline_run_id")
        == run.id
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["batch", None])
def test_explicit_batch_and_default_online_never_freeze_an_online_fallback(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, mode
) -> None:
    """显式 batch 与省略 mode 后默认 online 都不得带上 24 小时在线兜底。

    带上就意味着这一轮在 3 次批量尝试用尽或 24 小时到期后会真的走在线——在线价是
    批量的 1.845 倍，而运营明确选的是批量。
    """

    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import policy
    from app.main import BaselineRunCreateRequest

    requested = BaselineRunCreateRequest().inference_mode if mode is None else mode
    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _fallback_fixture(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch, name=f"fallback-{mode}"
    )
    try:
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode=requested)
        frozen = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)["inference_route"]
        assert frozen["mode"] == ("online" if mode is None else "batch")
        assert "fallback_policy" not in frozen
        routes = _runtime_routes(db, run)
        assert routes and all("fallback_policy" not in route for route in routes)
        for route in routes:
            assert policy({"inference_route": route}) is None
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_frozen_batch_run_cannot_be_switched_online_by_the_recovery_path(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """冻结快照里没有兜底策略，恢复路径就无权把传输改成在线。

    这是终点断言：`decide` 在 3 次批量尝试或 24 小时后会判 online，但只有
    `effective_route` 能把它变成真实端点，而它要求冻结运行时授权过。
    """

    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import (
        DEADLINE_SECONDS,
        decide,
        effective_route,
        policy,
    )

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _fallback_fixture(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    try:
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch")
        runtime = json.loads(
            db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_json
        )["runtime_context"]
        queued_at = datetime(2026, 9, 13, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        # +25 小时：既超过 24 小时截止，也远超 3 次批量尝试的场景。
        overdue = queued_at + timedelta(seconds=DEADLINE_SECONDS + 3600)
        assert decide(
            queued_at=queued_at,
            now=overdue,
            batch_count=3,
            online_count=0,
            online_limit=3,
        ).mode == "online"
        # 没有冻结兜底策略，任何传输覆写都无权进行——在线尤其不行。
        for override in ("online", "batch"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError) as error:
                effective_route(runtime, override)
            assert "not authorized" in str(error.value)
        # 不带覆写时仍是本轮冻结的那条批量路由。
        assert effective_route(runtime, None)["endpoint_id"] == BATCH_ENDPOINT
        assert effective_route(runtime, None)["mode"] == "batch"
        # 执行器据此走普通批量分支，整套兜底机制根本不会被启用。
        assert policy(runtime) is None
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_explicit_batch_does_not_need_a_verified_online_endpoint(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """兜底开关开着、但没有可用在线接入点时，显式批量照样能启动。

    原先这里会抛 batch_online_fallback_unavailable——那是给「批量+兜底」准备的门槛，
    本轮全程批量根本不需要在线接入点。
    """

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _fallback_fixture(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch, name="fallback-no-online", bind_online=False
    )
    try:
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="batch")
        frozen = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)["inference_route"]
        assert frozen["mode"] == "batch" and "fallback_policy" not in frozen
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_auto_mode_keeps_the_shared_online_fallback_unchanged(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """共享的 auto 策略不动：兜底开关开着时 auto 仍然冻结兜底。"""

    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import policy

    engine, db, baseline, workflow = _fallback_fixture(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch, name="fallback-auto"
    )
    try:
        run = _create(db, baseline, workflow, inference_mode="auto")
        frozen = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)["inference_route"]
        assert frozen["mode"] == "batch"
        assert frozen["fallback_policy"]["version"] == "batch-online-fallback-v1"
        assert frozen["fallback_policy"]["deadline_seconds"] == 86400
        assert frozen["fallback_policy"]["batch_max_attempts"] == 3
        assert frozen["fallback_policy"]["online_route"]["endpoint_id"] == ONLINE_ENDPOINT
        for route in _runtime_routes(db, run):
            assert policy({"inference_route": route}) is not None
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_shared_route_selection_keeps_fallback_for_other_callers(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """增量等其他调用方走的是 select_inference_route 默认路径，行为不变。"""

    from app.doubao_endpoints import select_inference_route

    engine, db, _, workflow = _fallback_fixture(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch, name="fallback-shared"
    )
    try:
        shared = select_inference_route(
            db, workflow.workflow_definition_id, mode="batch", offline=True
        )
        assert shared["fallback_policy"]["version"] == "batch-online-fallback-v1"
        opted_out = select_inference_route(
            db,
            workflow.workflow_definition_id,
            mode="batch",
            offline=True,
            allow_online_fallback=False,
        )
        assert "fallback_policy" not in opted_out
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_release_fee_verification.py =====
"""New paid rows do not invalidate the old-row preservation contract."""
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import pytest
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW


def test_frozen_fee_ids_allow_new_rows_but_reject_old_mutation_or_deletion(database):
    from app.release_fee_verification import capture_invocation_fees, verify_invocation_fees
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).baseline_run_id = 328
        db.commit()
        captured = capture_invocation_fees(db, baseline_run_id=328)
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=2, step_attempt_id=2,
            invocation_key='new', node_instance_id='prompt', baseline_run_id=328,
            provider='doubao', model_id='test', status='reserved', estimated_cost_micros=250))
        db.commit()
        report = verify_invocation_fees(db, baseline_run_id=328, captured=captured)
        assert report['old_count'] == 1 and report['new_count'] == 1
        assert report['new_rows'][0]['estimated_cost_micros'] == 250
        old = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, captured[0][0])
        old.estimated_cost_micros += 1
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='old invocation fees changed'):
            verify_invocation_fees(db, baseline_run_id=328, captured=captured)
        db.delete(old); db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='old invocation fees changed'):
            verify_invocation_fees(db, baseline_run_id=328, captured=captured)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_release_ops_gates.py =====
"""The release fence must take its per-release values as arguments.

Both constants these tests cover went stale in production without anyone
noticing: gated-acquisition.py pinned the baseline image of whichever release
last edited it, and stop-admission-base.py required exactly four evaluation
owners, which stopped being true when the topology moved from 3x32 to 6x16.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
OPS = ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "release-ops"


def _load(name: str):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        name.replace("-", "_"), OPS / f"{name}.py"
    )
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


class _Cursor:
    """Answers exactly the queries assert_idle issues, in order of matching."""

    def __init__(self, owners, *, schema_version=114, active=0, stray_reserved=0):
        self.owners = owners
        self.schema_version = schema_version
        self.active = active
        self.stray_reserved = stray_reserved
        self._rows: list = []

    def execute(self, sql, *_args):
        if "MAX(version) FROM schema_migrations" in sql:
            self._rows = [(self.schema_version,)]
        elif sql.startswith("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `"):
            self._rows = [(self.active,)]
        elif "status='reserved'" in sql:
            self._rows = [(self.stray_reserved,)]
        elif "MAX(finished_at)" in sql or "MAX(updated_at)" in sql:
            self._rows = [(None,)]
        elif "FROM automation_worker_statuses" in sql:
            self._rows = list(self.owners)
        else:  # pragma: no cover - a new query should fail loudly, not silently
            raise AssertionError("unexpected query: " + sql)

    def fetchone(self):
        return self._rows[0]

    def fetchall(self):
        return self._rows


class _Connection:
    def __init__(self, cursor):
        self._cursor = cursor

    def cursor(self):
        return self._cursor


def _owner_rows(evaluation: int, *, tick_at=None):
    tick_at = tick_at or datetime(2026, 9, 15, 1, 0, 0)
    payload = json.dumps({"runtime": "bounded_async", "active": 0})
    rows = [
        (f"evaluation:async-{index}", "idle", payload, 3, 3, tick_at)
        for index in range(evaluation)
    ]
    # Non-evaluation owners are present in production and must not be counted.
    rows.append(("auxiliary:host-1", "idle", "{}", 8, 8, tick_at))
    rows.append(("optimization:host-1", "disabled", "{}", 1, 1, tick_at))
    return rows


@pytest.mark.parametrize("expected,actual,accepted", [
    (7, 7, True),
    (4, 4, True),
    # The exact failure that blocked the 2026-09-15 release: the gate wanted the
    # old 3x32 count while the 6x16 topology was live.
    (4, 7, False),
    (7, 4, False),
    (7, 6, False),
    (7, 8, False),
])
def test_idle_proof_counts_the_topology_it_is_given(expected, actual, accepted):
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    connection = _Connection(_Cursor(_owner_rows(actual)))
    if accepted:
        gate.assert_idle(connection, expected, 114)
    else:
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="evaluation owners must prove idle"):
            gate.assert_idle(connection, expected, 114)


def test_idle_proof_still_rejects_a_busy_owner():
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    rows = _owner_rows(7)
    rows[0] = (rows[0][0], "running", rows[0][2], 3, 3, rows[0][5])
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="owner has not returned"):
        gate.assert_idle(_Connection(_Cursor(rows)), 7, 114)


def test_idle_proof_still_rejects_active_work_before_counting_owners():
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    cursor = _Cursor(_owner_rows(7), active=1)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="active work"):
        gate.assert_idle(_Connection(cursor), 7, 114)


def test_idle_proof_still_rejects_an_unexpected_reserved_invocation():
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    cursor = _Cursor(_owner_rows(7), stray_reserved=1)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="paid invocations still in flight"):
        gate.assert_idle(_Connection(cursor), 7, 114)


def test_idle_proof_rejects_a_sample_older_than_the_last_settlement():
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    stale = datetime(2026, 9, 15, 1, 0, 0)
    cursor = _Cursor(_owner_rows(7, tick_at=stale))

    original = cursor.execute

    def execute(sql, *args):
        original(sql, *args)
        if "MAX(finished_at)" in sql:
            cursor._rows = [(stale + timedelta(seconds=5),)]

    cursor.execute = execute
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="idle proof predates runtime settlement"):
        gate.assert_idle(_Connection(cursor), 7, 114)


def test_locked_owner_freshness_is_captured_before_slow_settlement_scans():
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    cursor = _Cursor(_owner_rows(7))
    original = cursor.execute
    scanned = False
    owner_reads = 0

    def execute(sql, *args):
        nonlocal scanned, owner_reads
        original(sql, *args)
        if "MAX(finished_at)" in sql or "MAX(updated_at)" in sql:
            # Model >30s of elapsed scan time while WRITE locks prevent every
            # worker from updating its heartbeat. No actual sleep/DB needed.
            scanned = True
        if "FROM automation_worker_statuses" in sql:
            owner_reads += 1
            if scanned:
                cursor._rows = []

    cursor.execute = execute
    gate.assert_idle(_Connection(cursor), 7, 114)
    assert scanned and owner_reads == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("tick_age,heartbeat_age", [(31, 3), (3, 31), (-1, 3)])
def test_initial_stale_or_future_heartbeat_is_still_rejected(tick_age, heartbeat_age):
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    rows = _owner_rows(7)
    rows[0] = (*rows[0][:3], tick_age, heartbeat_age, rows[0][5])
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
        gate.assert_idle(_Connection(_Cursor(rows)), 7, 114)


def test_neither_script_hardcodes_a_release_specific_constant():
    fence = (OPS / "gated-acquisition.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    gate = (OPS / "stop-admission-base.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    # A literal image digest here is what drifted release after release.
    assert not re.search(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}", fence)
    assert "--expected-baseline-image" in fence
    # The owner count must never be compared against a literal again.
    assert not re.search(r"evaluation == \d", gate)
    assert "--expected-owners" in gate
    # The fence has to forward it, or the gate would fail closed on every run.
    assert "'--expected-owners', str(args.expected_owners)" in fence
    assert "'--expected-schema-version', str(args.expected_schema_version)" in fence
    schema_proof = gate.split("SELECT MAX(version) FROM schema_migrations", 1)[1].split("for table,", 1)[0]
    assert not re.search(r"cursor.fetchone\(\)\[0\] == \d", schema_proof)


def test_both_per_release_values_are_required_arguments():
    fence = (OPS / "gated-acquisition.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    gate = (OPS / "stop-admission-base.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for source, flag in ((fence, "--expected-baseline-image"),
                         (fence, "--expected-owners"),
                         (gate, "--expected-owners"),
                         (fence, "--expected-schema-version"),
                         (gate, "--expected-schema-version")):
        pattern = r"add_argument\('" + re.escape(flag) + r"', required=True"
        assert re.search(pattern, source), flag + " must be required, not defaulted"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("actual,expected,accepted", [
    (126, 126, True), (127, 127, True),
    (126, 114, False), (126, 127, False), (127, 126, False),
])
def test_schema_version_is_pinned_not_a_forward_compatibility_range(actual, expected, accepted):
    gate = _load("stop-admission-base")
    connection = _Connection(_Cursor(_owner_rows(7), schema_version=actual))
    if accepted:
        gate.assert_idle(connection, 7, expected)
    else:
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="unexpected schema version"):
            gate.assert_idle(connection, 7, expected)


def test_first_module_cutover_needs_exact_command_and_reviewed_file_digest(tmp_path):
    import hashlib
    fence = _load('gated-acquisition')
    entry = tmp_path / 'first-module-cutover.py'
    entry.write_text('# reviewed test entry; not executable deployment\n')
    digest = hashlib.sha256(entry.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    command = ['/usr/bin/python3', str(entry), '--apply']
    fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, command, digest)
    for changed in [command + ['--skip-gate'], ['python3', str(entry), '--apply'],
                    ['/usr/bin/python3', '/tmp/another.py', '--apply']]:
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError): fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, changed, digest)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError): fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, command)
    entry.write_text('# changed after review\n')
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match='changed after review'):
        fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, command, digest)


def test_first_module_cutover_refuses_symlink_and_legacy_path_is_unchanged(tmp_path):
    import hashlib
    fence = _load('gated-acquisition')
    original = tmp_path / 'other.py'; original.write_text('# original\n')
    entry = tmp_path / 'first-module-cutover.py'; entry.symlink_to(original)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match='regular'):
        fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, ['/usr/bin/python3', str(entry), '--apply'], hashlib.sha256(original.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
    legacy = [str(tmp_path / 'source/deploy/server/deploy.sh'), 'env', '--existing-schema', 'backup', '--prebuilt-image', 'image.tar', '--prebuilt-sha256', 'digest']
    fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, legacy)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError): fence.validate_deployment_command(tmp_path, legacy + ['extra'])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_release_window.py =====
"""发布窗口：能等的暂存，在飞的等它收尾，恢复只放自己那批。

2026-09-16 的发布最后取消了 75 条在途运行才过得了准入闸门，因为自然排空要 20 小时。
那批运行绝大多数卡在批量重试的退避计时器上——没有在飞调用，全部进度都在检查点里。
"""
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.release_window import (
    ReleaseWindowError, held_run_ids, release_hold, release_resume,
)
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close

RELEASE = "74b9090be524e4e87d789022e41bcf3f7bac5615"


@pytest.fixture
def world(monkeypatch):
    """真的通过入口事件建出一条运行，再按需克隆——ProductionRun 的必填列很多。"""

    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _headers, _payload

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
                           json=_payload(event_id="rw-seed", asset_id=asset.id)).status_code == 200
        yield db
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def _run(db, *, status="retryable", key):
    template = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
    values = {c.name: getattr(template, c.name)
              for c in ProductionRun.__table__.columns if c.name != "id"}
    values.update(run_key=key, idempotency_key=key + "-idem",
                  status=status, held_by_release=None)
    clone = ProductionRun(**values)
    db.add(clone)
    db.commit()
    return clone


def _invocation(db, run, *, status, error_code=""):
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    assert attempt is not None, "种子运行应当带着真实的步骤尝试"
    return WorkflowModelInvocation(
        production_run_id=run.id, step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
        invocation_key=f"inv-{status}-{error_code or 'ok'}", node_instance_id="prompt_a",
        status=status, error_code=error_code, provider="doubao", model_id="m")


def _seed_run(db, *, status):
    """把 fixture 建出来的那条本体设成指定状态，避免每个用例都多一条噪音运行。"""

    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id))
    run.status = status
    db.commit()
    return run


def test_a_run_parked_on_a_backoff_timer_is_held(world):
    run = _run(world, status="retryable", key="idle-1")
    result = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in result.held
    world.expire_all()
    stored = world.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
    assert stored.status == "paused" and stored.held_by_release == RELEASE
    assert held_run_ids(world, release=RELEASE) == sorted(result.held)


def test_new_window_does_not_resume_qwen_held_by_interrupted_window(world):
    old = _seed_run(world, status='paused')
    old.held_by_release = RELEASE
    world.commit()
    new = _run(world, status='retryable', key='new-window-incremental')
    marker = RELEASE + '-20260924T030000Z'
    release_hold(world, release=marker, actor='ops', reason='recovery window')
    world.commit()
    assert held_run_ids(world, release=marker) == [new.id]
    release_resume(world, release=marker, actor='ops')
    world.expire_all()
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, old.id).status == 'paused'
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, old.id).held_by_release == RELEASE


def test_a_run_holding_a_reserved_paid_call_is_left_to_settle(world):
    """在飞且已计费的调用不能暂存——恢复会重跑一遍并二次计费。"""

    run = _seed_run(world, status="running")
    world.add(_invocation(world, run, status="reserved"))
    world.commit()
    result = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in result.must_settle and run.id not in result.held
    world.expire_all()
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "running"


def test_a_run_with_a_worker_holding_a_step_is_left_to_settle(world):
    run = _seed_run(world, status="running")
    attempt = world.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                           .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    attempt.status = "leased"
    world.commit()
    result = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in result.must_settle


def test_resume_only_releases_what_this_release_held(world):
    """运营自己暂停的运行不能被发布顺带放出来。"""

    mine = _run(world, status="retryable", key="mine-1")
    theirs = _run(world, status="paused", key="theirs-1")
    release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert mine.id in held_run_ids(world, release=RELEASE)
    assert theirs.id not in held_run_ids(world, release=RELEASE)
    world.expire_all()
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, theirs.id).held_by_release is None
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, theirs.id).status == "paused", "运营暂停的运行原样不动"


def test_a_terminal_unknown_outcome_does_not_count_as_in_flight(world):
    """model_invocation_outcome_unknown 是终态失败，不是还有调用在飞。

    生产上这类行有一千多条，把它们算成在飞会让整个机制退化回「什么都动不了」。
    """

    run = _seed_run(world, status="retryable")
    world.add(_invocation(world, run, status="failed",
                          error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown"))
    world.commit()
    result = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in result.held


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field,value", [("release", "  "), ("reason", "  ")])
def test_hold_refuses_to_run_without_its_evidence(world, field, value):
    kwargs = {"release": RELEASE, "actor": "ops", "reason": "发布窗口"}
    kwargs[field] = value
    with pytest.raises(ReleaseWindowError):
        release_hold(world, **kwargs)


def test_resume_puts_the_held_runs_back(world):
    run = _seed_run(world, status="retryable")
    held = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in held.held
    release_resume(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops")
    world.expire_all()
    assert world.get(ProductionRun, run.id).held_by_release is None
    assert held_run_ids(world, release=RELEASE) == [], "标记必须清干净，否则下次发布会重复放"


def _batch_pool_and_call(db, run, *, age_hours=0.0):
    """一个批量配额池，和一次挂在它上面的 ep-bi- 调用（决定 hold 要不要给它登记维护）。"""

    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    from app.models import ModelQuotaPool
    pool = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaPool))
    if pool is None:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key="rw-batch", name="rw-batch", provider="doubao",
                              scope_kind="account", scope_identifier="rw", max_concurrency=8,
                              lease_seconds=900, created_by="test")
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
    # 只有 fixture 的本体带着真实步骤尝试；克隆出来的运行拿来被暂存。
    seed = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id))
    call = _invocation(db, seed, status="failed", error_code="model_provider_429")
    call.endpoint_id = "ep-bi-rw"
    call.quota_pool_id = pool.id
    call.created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=age_hours)
    db.add(call)
    db.commit()
    return pool


def _maintenance_events(db):
    from app.models import AuditEvent
    return [(e.action, e.event_key) for e in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(
        AuditEvent.action.in_(["pacing_maintenance_pause", "pacing_maintenance_resume"]))
        .order_by(AuditEvent.id))]


def test_hold_opens_one_pacing_maintenance_interval_per_recent_batch_pool_and_resume_closes_it(world):
    """2026-09-16: 6 次冷启动回 2 RPM 有 4 次落在发布点，每次约 35 分钟才爬回来。

    发布窗口从没给共享提交控制器登记过维护区间，二十几分钟的排空加部署被当成空闲。
    """

    from app.release_window import pacing_maintenance_incident
    run = _run(world, status="retryable", key="maint-1")
    pool = _batch_pool_and_call(world, run)
    key = f"batch-pacing:pool:{pool.id}:{pacing_maintenance_incident(RELEASE)}"
    first = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    # 窗口脚本每 30 秒重复 hold；区间只开一次。
    release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in first.held
    assert _maintenance_events(world) == [("pacing_maintenance_pause", key + ":pause")]
    from app.models import AuditEvent
    hold_event = world.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.category == "release_window",
                                                       AuditEvent.action == "hold").order_by(AuditEvent.id))
    import json
    assert json.loads(hold_event.payload_json)["pacing_maintenance_pools"] == [pool.id]
    payload = release_resume(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops")
    assert payload["pacing_maintenance_pools"] == [pool.id]
    assert _maintenance_events(world) == [("pacing_maintenance_pause", key + ":pause"),
                                          ("pacing_maintenance_resume", key + ":resume")]


def test_hold_records_no_maintenance_for_a_pool_without_recent_batch_calls(world):
    run = _run(world, status="retryable", key="maint-2")
    _batch_pool_and_call(world, run, age_hours=25)
    release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert _maintenance_events(world) == []
    # 没有 pause 的 resume 不发明起点。
    assert release_resume(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops")["pacing_maintenance_pools"] == []
    assert _maintenance_events(world) == []


def _block_on_manual_node(db, run):
    """人工处理节点：一个步骤 blocked，其余路线仍是 pending（未选择）。"""

    attempts = list(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                               .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
                               .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id)))
    assert len(attempts) >= 2, "种子运行应当有多个步骤"
    attempts[0].status = "blocked"
    for other in attempts[1:]:
        other.status = "pending"
    # 与 block_runtime_step 一致：阻塞时同运行的排队派发全部取消。
    from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem
    for dispatch in db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id.in_([a.id for a in attempts]))):
        dispatch.status = "canceled"
    run.status = "blocked"
    db.commit()
    return attempts


def test_resume_returns_a_manual_review_run_to_blocked_not_the_unchosen_route(world):
    """hold 把 blocked 改成 paused；恢复不能沿人工未选择的路线继续跑。"""

    from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem
    run = _seed_run(world, status="blocked")
    attempts = _block_on_manual_node(world, run)
    held = release_hold(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops", reason="发布窗口")
    world.commit()
    assert run.id in held.held, "闸门把 blocked 算活跃，必须能暂存"
    payload = release_resume(world, release=RELEASE, actor="ops")
    world.expire_all()
    stored = world.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
    assert stored.status == "blocked"
    assert stored.held_by_release is None
    assert payload["failed_count"] == 0
    assert run.id in payload["restored_blocked"]
    queued = world.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
        RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id.in_([a.id for a in attempts]),
        RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued")).all()
    assert queued == [], "未选择的路线不能被重新派发"


def test_resume_from_checkpoint_refuses_manual_review_regardless_of_run_status(world):
    from app.workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError, resume_from_checkpoint
    run = _seed_run(world, status="blocked")
    _block_on_manual_node(world, run)
    run.status = "paused"  # e.g. an older hold already rewrote it
    world.commit()
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as error:
        resume_from_checkpoint(world, run.id)
    assert error.value.code == "run_manual_review_required"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_release_window_entrypoints.py =====
"""发布窗口的两个运维入口：容器内的 release-window.py 和宿主上的 repin-intake.py。

它们不在应用的导入路径上，测的是「发布当天真正会依赖的那几件事」——退出码的含义、
镜像 id 的守卫、以及 README 里承诺的步骤确实存在。2026-09-16 那次发布，遗漏的正是
runtime.env 与进料钉子这两步，没有任何测试或告警会提醒。
"""
import pathlib
import re

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
WINDOW = ROOT / "scripts" / "release-window.py"
REPIN = ROOT / "deploy" / "release" / "repin-intake.py"
README = ROOT / "deploy" / "release" / "README.md"


def test_the_in_container_script_ships_inside_the_image():
    """必须在 scripts/ 下：镜像只 COPY backend/app、scripts、frontend/dist，不含 deploy/。"""

    assert WINDOW.is_file()
    assert (ROOT / "deploy" / "release" / "release-window.py").exists() is False


def test_hold_reports_still_settling_with_a_distinct_exit_code():
    """「还有在飞调用」必须和「失败」区分开，否则发布脚本只能靠解析输出来判断。"""

    source = WINDOW.read_text()
    assert "EXIT_STILL_SETTLING = 75" in source
    assert "return EXIT_STILL_SETTLING if result.must_settle else 0" in source

===== FILE backend/tests/test_response_format_mode.py =====
"""response_format_mode：json_object 只发宽松 JSON，合同校验留在平台。"""
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.doubao import DoubaoClient
from app.prompt_output_contract import PromptOutputContract

SCHEMA = {"type": "object", "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}}, "required": ["score"],
          "additionalProperties": False}
CONTRACT = PromptOutputContract(mode="json_schema", name="x", strict=True, schema=SCHEMA)


def _client(mode: str | None) -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    fields = dict(protocol="openai_chat", provider="local_qwen", model_id="qwen3.8-27b",
                  max_tokens=1000, temperature=0.1, structured_output=True, thinking_mode="auto")
    if mode is not None:
        fields["response_format_mode"] = mode
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(**fields)
    return client


def _full(mode):
    payload: dict[str, object] = {"messages": [{"role": "system", "content": "评分。"}, {"role": "user", "content": "x"}]}
    _client(mode)._generation_options(payload, output_budget=500, reasoning_effort=None,
                                      structured_output=True, output_contract=CONTRACT)
    return payload


def _payload(mode):
    return _full(mode)["response_format"]


def test_default_and_contract_mode_send_the_frozen_schema():
    assert _payload(None)["type"] == "json_schema"
    assert _payload("contract")["json_schema"]["schema"] == SCHEMA


def test_json_object_mode_drops_the_grammar_but_keeps_json():
    assert _payload("json_object") == {"type": "json_object"}


def test_json_object_mode_carries_the_schema_in_the_system_prompt():
    """撤掉语法约束后合同必须进提示词：run 362 全部漏 required 字段就是没带。"""
    import json as _json

    system = _full("json_object")["messages"][0]["content"]
    assert system.startswith("评分。") and "【输出格式硬约束】" in system
    # 骨架用尖括号占位符，不是可照抄的合法 JSON；原始 schema 不出现（run 363 有 20% 把 schema 抄回来）
    assert '"score": <整数>' in system and "$ 必填：score" in system and "不要输出未列出的字段" in system
    assert "additionalProperties" not in system and _json.dumps(SCHEMA, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) not in system
    assert _full("contract")["messages"][0]["content"] == "评分。"


def test_schema_guide_renders_nested_enum_range_and_arrays():
    from app.prompt_output_contract import render_schema_guide

    schema = {"type": "object", "required": ["level", "dims"], "additionalProperties": False, "properties": {
        "level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L2"]},
        "score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100},
        "note": {"type": ["string", "null"], "description": "一句话"},
        "dims": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object", "required": ["key"], "properties": {"key": {"type": "string"}, "grade": {"type": "number"}}}},
        "version": {"const": "v3"},
    }}
    guide = render_schema_guide(schema)
    assert '"level": <"L1"|"L2">' in guide and '"score": <整数 0-100>' in guide
    assert '"note": <字符串|null>' in guide and '"version": "v3"' in guide
    assert '"dims": [{' in guide and '"grade": <数值>' in guide and "$.dims[] 必填：key" in guide
    assert "$.note：一句话" in guide
    import json as _json
    with __import__("pytest").raises(ValueError):
        _json.loads(guide.split("：", 1)[1].split("字段要求")[0])  # 骨架不是合法 JSON


def test_v3_validates_response_format_mode():
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3
    from tests.test_workflow_v3 import _valid_manifest

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node.get("kind") == "prompt")
    prompt["config"]["response_format_mode"] = "yaml"
    assert "workflow_model_response_format_mode_invalid" in {e.code for e in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    prompt["config"]["response_format_mode"] = "json_object"
    codes = {e.code for e in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert "workflow_model_response_format_mode_invalid" not in codes
    assert "workflow_node_config_field_unknown" not in codes


def test_prepared_client_config_threads_the_mode():
    from app.workflow_model_executor import _prepared_client_config

    current = SimpleNamespace(encrypted_api_key="k")
    frozen = {"provider": "local_qwen", "max_tokens": 8192, "temperature": 0.1, "structured_output": True}
    node = {"max_tokens": 1000, "temperature": 0.1, "structured_output": True, "thinking_level": "none",
            "response_format_mode": "json_object"}
