    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _seed_inputs(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[ModelConfig, PromptVersion, PromptVersion, SamplingPolicy]:
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="Routed Test Model",
        provider="doubao",
        base_url="https://example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="routed-test-model",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted-key-never-snapshot",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        structured_output=True,
        high_risk_review_enabled=True,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="Route A",
        version="route-A-v1",
        system_prompt="System A",
        user_prompt="User A",
        rubric_version="route-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="Space B",
        version="space-B-v1",
        system_prompt="System B",
        user_prompt="User B",
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    sampling = SamplingPolicy(
        id=1,
        revision=1,
        sample_rate=10,
        low_confidence_threshold=0.7,
        medium_confidence_threshold=0.9,
        cold_start_required_count=5,
        high_level_required_from=4,
    )
    db.add_all([model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling])
    db.commit()
    return model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling


def _registry(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[
    DimensionRoutePolicy,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
]:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionRoutePolicy).where(
            DimensionRoutePolicy.policy_key == ROUTE_POLICY_KEY,
            DimensionRoutePolicy.version == ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION,
        )
    )
    space = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        )
    )
    core = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == CORE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == CORE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    product = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    assert policy and space and core and product
    return policy, space, core, product


def _profile_set(
    db: Session,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion,
    *,
    reverse: bool = False,
    execution_context: str = "calibration",
) -> dict:
    _, space, core, product = _registry(db)
    profiles = [
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="space",
            schema=space,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
        ),
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="common",
            schema=core,
            prompt_b=None,
        ),
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="product",
            schema=product,
            prompt_b=None,
        ),
    ]
    if reverse:
        profiles.reverse()
    return build_evaluation_profile_set(
        profiles=profiles,
        execution_context=execution_context,
        default_profile_key="common",
    )


def test_v3_calibration_bundle_freezes_all_profiles(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, _, _, _ = _registry(db)
    profile_set = _profile_set(db, prompt_b)
    bundle = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=profile_set,
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()

    assert bundle.strategy_schema_version == ROUTED_STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION
    assert bundle.prompt_b_version is None
    assert (
        bundle.resolved_schema_contract_version
        == ROUTED_SCHEMA_CONTRACT_VERSION
    )
    assert bundle.dimension_route_policy_id == (
        f"{ROUTE_POLICY_KEY}@{ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION}"
    )
    stored_profiles = json.loads(bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot)
    assert list(stored_profiles["profiles"]) == [
        "common",
        "product",
        "space",
    ]
    assert stored_profiles["execution_context"] == "calibration"
    assert (
        stored_profiles["profiles"]["product"]["release_gate"][
            "publishing_blocked"
        ]
        is True
    )
    assert stored_profiles["profiles"]["product"]["prompt_b"] is None
    snapshot = json.loads(
        build_strategy_snapshot(
            bundle,
            prompt_a,
            None,
            sampling,
        )
    )
    assert snapshot["schema_version"] == ROUTED_STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION
    assert snapshot["prompt_b"] is None
    assert snapshot["evaluation_profile_set_snapshot"] == stored_profiles
    assert (
        snapshot["dimension_route_policy_snapshot"]["policy_key"]
        == ROUTE_POLICY_KEY
    )


def test_v3_profile_order_is_canonical_and_reused(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, _, _, _ = _registry(db)
    first = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=_profile_set(db, prompt_b),
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    second = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=_profile_set(
            db,
            prompt_b,
            reverse=True,
        ),
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    assert second.id == first.id
    assert second.canonical_hash == first.canonical_hash


def test_v3_prompt_rotation_changes_bundle_hash(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, _, _, _ = _registry(db)
    first = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=_profile_set(db, prompt_b),
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    rotated = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="Space B v2",
        version="space-B-v2",
        system_prompt="System B changed",
        user_prompt="User B",
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    db.add(rotated)
    db.commit()
    second = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=_profile_set(db, rotated),
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    assert second.id != first.id
    assert second.canonical_hash != first.canonical_hash


def test_v3_rejects_production_candidate_policy(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, _, _, _ = _registry(db)
    profile_set = _profile_set(
        db,
        prompt_b,
        execution_context="production",
    )
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="生产 Bundle 只能冻结已发布的生产路由策略",
    ):
        get_or_create_routed_bundle(
            db=db,
            model_config=model,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            route_policy=policy,
            evaluation_profile_set=profile_set,
            engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
            risk_review_version=None,
            sampling_policy=sampling,
        )


def test_v3_contract_rejects_tampered_profile_hash(db: Session) -> None:
    _model, _prompt_a, prompt_b, _sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    profile_set = _profile_set(db, prompt_b)
    profile_set["profiles"]["space"]["prompt_b"]["system_prompt"] = (
        "tampered"
    )
    policy, _, _, _ = _registry(db)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="规范哈希无效"):
        get_or_create_routed_bundle(
            db=db,
            model_config=_model,
            prompt_a=_prompt_a,
            route_policy=policy,
            evaluation_profile_set=profile_set,
            engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
            risk_review_version=None,
            sampling_policy=_sampling,
        )


def test_v2_bundle_remains_unchanged_after_migration_29(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1",
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    assert bundle.strategy_schema_version == "strategy-bundle-v2"
    assert bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot is None
    assert bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot is None


def test_migration_29_trigger_rejects_malformed_v3(db: Session) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        db.execute(
            StrategyBundle.__table__.insert().values(
                canonical_hash="f" * 64,
                strategy_schema_version="strategy-bundle-v3",
                model_id="bad",
                model_config_snapshot="{}",
                prompt_a_version="A",
                prompt_b_version=None,
                rubric_version="R",
                engine_version="E",
                agent_plan_version="P",
                dimension_route_policy_id="bad@v1",
                dimension_schema_set_snapshot='{"schemas":[{}]}',
                label_field_set_snapshot='{"sets":[]}',
                resolved_schema_contract_version="v2",
                dimension_route_policy_snapshot="{}",
                evaluation_profile_set_snapshot="{}",
            )
        )
        db.flush()


def test_migration_29_upgrades_real_v2_shape_without_rewriting(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'pre-v29.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
                    version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
                    applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            for migration in MIGRATIONS:
                if migration.version >= 29:
                    break
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) "
                    "VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (migration.version, migration.name),
                )
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                CREATE TABLE strategy_bundles (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    canonical_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    strategy_schema_version VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
                    model_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
                    model_config_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
                    prompt_a_version VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
                    prompt_b_version VARCHAR(40),
                    rubric_version VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
                    engine_version VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
                    sampling_policy_revision INTEGER,
                    risk_review_version VARCHAR(40),
                    agent_plan_version VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
                    dimension_route_policy_id VARCHAR(100),
                    dimension_schema_set_snapshot TEXT,
                    label_field_set_snapshot TEXT,
                    resolved_schema_contract_version VARCHAR(80)
                )
            """)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                INSERT INTO strategy_bundles (
                    id, canonical_hash, strategy_schema_version,
                    model_id, model_config_snapshot,
                    prompt_a_version, prompt_b_version,
                    rubric_version, engine_version,
                    agent_plan_version, dimension_route_policy_id,
                    dimension_schema_set_snapshot,
                    label_field_set_snapshot,
                    resolved_schema_contract_version
                ) VALUES (
                    1, ?, 'strategy-bundle-v2',
                    'model', '{}', 'A1', 'B1', 'R1', 'E1', 'P1',
                    'space-static-by-scoring-profile-v1',
                    '{"schemas":[{"schema_key":"space"}]}',
                    '{"sets":[]}',
                    'dimension-resolution-v1'
                )
            """, ("a" * 64,))

            migration_29 = next(
                item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 29
            )
            migration_29.up(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
                (migration_29.version, migration_29.name),
            )
            migration_29.up(connection)

            columns = {
                row[1]
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA table_info(strategy_bundles)"
                )
            }
            assert {
                "dimension_route_policy_snapshot",
                "evaluation_profile_set_snapshot",
            } <= columns
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT strategy_schema_version, "
                "dimension_route_policy_snapshot, "
                "evaluation_profile_set_snapshot "
                "FROM strategy_bundles WHERE id=1"
            ).one() == ("strategy-bundle-v2", None, None)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == 29
            with pytest.raises(
                IntegrityError,
                match="routed dimension contract is invalid",
            ):
                connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                    INSERT INTO strategy_bundles (
                        id, canonical_hash, strategy_schema_version,
                        model_id, model_config_snapshot,
                        prompt_a_version, prompt_b_version,
                        rubric_version, engine_version,
                        agent_plan_version, dimension_route_policy_id,
                        dimension_schema_set_snapshot,
                        label_field_set_snapshot,
                        resolved_schema_contract_version,
                        dimension_route_policy_snapshot,
                        evaluation_profile_set_snapshot
                    ) VALUES (
                        2, ?, 'strategy-bundle-v3',
                        'model', '{}', 'A1', NULL, 'R1', 'E1', 'P1',
                        'bad@v1', '{"schemas":[{}]}', '{"sets":[]}',
                        'dimension-route-resolution-v2', '{}', '{}'
                    )
                """, ("b" * 64,))
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

        high_level_required_from=4,
    )
    db.add_all([model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling])
    db.commit()
    return model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling


def _registry(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[
    DimensionRoutePolicy,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
]:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionRoutePolicy).where(
            DimensionRoutePolicy.policy_key == ROUTE_POLICY_KEY,
            DimensionRoutePolicy.version == ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION,
        )
    )
    space = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        )
    )
    core = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == CORE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == CORE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    product = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    assert policy and space and core and product
    return policy, space, core, product


def _bundle(
    db: Session,
    *,
    include_product_prompt: bool,
) -> tuple[
    StrategyBundle,
    PromptVersion,
    SamplingPolicy,
]:
    model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, space, core, product = _registry(db)
    profiles = [
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="space",
            schema=space,
            prompt_b=space_b,
        ),
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="common",
            schema=core,
            prompt_b=None,
        ),
        build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            profile_key="product",
            schema=product,
            prompt_b=product_b if include_product_prompt else None,
        ),
    ]
    profile_set = build_evaluation_profile_set(
        profiles=profiles,
        execution_context="calibration",
        default_profile_key="common",
    )
    bundle = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=profile_set,
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    return bundle, prompt_a, sampling


def _precheck(
    *,
    scope_status: str = "in_scope",
    primary_category: str = "住宅设计",
    primary_confidence: float = 0.92,
    scene_scope: str = "full_space",
    white_background: str = "no",
    quality_severity: str = "normal",
    needs_review: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": scope_status,
            "primary_category": primary_category,
            "primary_confidence": primary_confidence,
        },
        "scene_scope": {"type": scene_scope},
        "media_form": {
            "white_background_product": {
                "status": white_background,
            }
        },
        "image_quality": {
            "quality_severity": quality_severity,
        },
        "needs_review": needs_review,
        "must_not_freeze": {"raw_model_payload": "not part of route input"},
    }


def _rehash_without_canonical_hash(payload: dict) -> None:
    payload["canonical_hash"] = canonical_hash(
        {
            key: value
            for key, value in payload.items()
            if key != "canonical_hash"
        }
    )


def _rehash_profile_set(payload: dict, profile_key: str) -> None:
    _rehash_without_canonical_hash(payload["profiles"][profile_key])
    _rehash_without_canonical_hash(payload)


def test_space_route_selects_frozen_space_profile(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=False,
    )
    resolved = resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
        bundle=bundle,
        precheck=_precheck(),
    )
    profile = resolved["resolved_evaluation_profile"]
    assert resolved["format_version"] == RESOLVED_PROFILE_FORMAT_VERSION
    assert resolved["resolution_status"] == "resolved"
    assert profile["profile_key"] == "space"
    assert profile["prompt_b"]["version"] == "space-B-v1"
    assert resolved["blocked_reasons"] == []
    assert "must_not_freeze" not in resolved["route_input_snapshot"]

) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(

    assert resolved["resolved_evaluation_profile"]["profile_key"] == "product"
    assert resolved["blocked_reasons"] == ["prompt_contract_missing"]
    assert resolved["needs_review"] is True

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("precheck", "expected_status", "expected_profile"),
    [
        (
            _precheck(
                scope_status="out_of_scope",
                primary_category="平面设计",
                scene_scope="uncertain",
            ),
            "core_fallback",
            "common",
        ),
        (
            _precheck(
                scope_status="out_of_scope",
                primary_category="意向图",
                scene_scope="uncertain",
            ),
            "core_fallback",
            "common",
        ),
        (
            _precheck(quality_severity="unusable"),
            "unassessable",
            None,
        ),
    ],
)
def test_fallback_and_unassessable_are_explicit(
    db: Session,
    precheck: dict,
    expected_status: str,
    expected_profile: str | None,
) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=False,
    )
    resolved = resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
        bundle=bundle,
        precheck=precheck,
    )
    profile = resolved["resolved_evaluation_profile"]
    assert resolved["resolution_status"] == expected_status
    assert (
        profile["profile_key"]
        if isinstance(profile, dict)
        else None
    ) == expected_profile
    assert resolved["needs_review"] is True


def test_resolution_is_repeatable_from_frozen_bundle(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    precheck = _precheck(
        scope_status="boundary",
        primary_category="灯具照明",
        scene_scope="object_only",
    )
    expected = canonical_json(
        resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            bundle=bundle,
            precheck=precheck,
        )
    )
    assert {
        canonical_json(
            resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                bundle=bundle,
                precheck=precheck,
            )
        )
        for _ in range(100)
    } == {expected}


def test_profile_storage_order_does_not_change_resolution(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    precheck = _precheck(
        scope_status="boundary",
        primary_category="灯具照明",
        scene_scope="object_only",
    )
    expected = canonical_json(
        resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
            bundle=bundle,
            precheck=precheck,
        )
    )
    original = bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot
    payload = json.loads(original)
    payload["profiles"] = {
        key: payload["profiles"][key]
        for key in reversed(list(payload["profiles"]))
    }
    bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = json.dumps(
        payload,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    try:
        assert canonical_json(
            resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                bundle=bundle,
                precheck=precheck,
            )
        ) == expected
    finally:
        bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = original

    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(

            system_prompt="future system",
            user_prompt="future user",
            rubric_version="future-rubric",
            status="published",

def test_tampered_route_policy_body_is_rejected(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    original = bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot
    payload = json.loads(original)
    payload["definition"]["tampered"] = True
    bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot = canonical_json(payload)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(
            RoutedStrategyContractError,
            match="路由策略规范哈希无效",
        ):
            resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                bundle=bundle,
                precheck=_precheck(),
            )
    finally:
        bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot = original


def test_route_policy_cannot_expand_snapshot_input_allowlist(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    original = bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot
    payload = json.loads(original)
    payload["definition"]["input_contract"]["allowed_paths"].append(
        "must_not_freeze.raw_model_payload"
    )
    payload["canonical_hash"] = canonical_hash(payload["definition"])
    bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot = canonical_json(payload)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(
            RoutedStrategyContractError,
            match="允许输入字段不符合安全合同",
        ):
            resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                bundle=bundle,
                precheck=_precheck(),
            )
    finally:
        bundle.dimension_route_policy_snapshot = original


def test_tampered_profile_hash_is_rejected(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    original = bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot
    payload = json.loads(original)
    payload["profiles"]["product"]["canonical_hash"] = "0" * 64
    _rehash_without_canonical_hash(payload)
    bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = canonical_json(payload)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(
            RoutedStrategyContractError,
            match="EvaluationProfile product 规范哈希无效",
        ):
            resolve_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                bundle=bundle,
                precheck=_precheck(
                    primary_category="软装家具",
                    scene_scope="object_only",
                ),
            )
    finally:
        bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = original

def test_full_resolution_snapshot_is_hash_replayable(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    payload = json.loads(
        build_routed_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            sampling_policy=sampling,
            precheck=_precheck(
                scope_status="boundary",
                primary_category="软装饰品",
                scene_scope="object_only",
            ),
            resolution_timestamp=datetime(
                2026,
                7,
                31,
                1,
                2,
                3,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
        )
    )
    resolution_keys = {
        "format_version",
        "execution_context",
        "route_policy_hash",
        "route_input_snapshot",
        "route_decision_snapshot",
        "resolution_status",
        "needs_review",
        "blocked_reasons",
        "strategy_bundle_id",
        "strategy_bundle_hash",
        "resolved_evaluation_profile_key",
        "resolved_evaluation_profile_hash",
        "resolved_dimension_schema_key",
        "resolved_dimension_schema_version",
        "resolved_dimension_schema_hash",
        "resolved_dimensions_snapshot",
        "resolved_prompt_b_id",
        "resolved_prompt_b_version",
        "resolved_prompt_b_hash",
        "resolved_label_field_set_hash",
        "resolution_timestamp",
    }
    resolution = {key: payload[key] for key in resolution_keys}
    assert payload["resolved_snapshot_hash"] == canonical_hash(resolution)
    assert payload["schema_version"] == "strategy-bundle-v3"
    assert payload["prompt_b"] is None
    assert payload["resolved_evaluation_profile_key"] == "product"
    assert payload["resolved_prompt_b_version"] == (
        "product-B-v0.1-candidate.1"
    )


def test_resolution_snapshot_rejects_tampered_bundle_hash(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    original = bundle.canonical_hash
    bundle.canonical_hash = "0" * 64
    try:
        with pytest.raises(
            ValueError,
            match="StrategyBundle 不一致",
        ):
            build_routed_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
                bundle=bundle,
                prompt_a=prompt_a,
                sampling_policy=sampling,
                precheck=_precheck(),
                resolution_timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
    finally:
        bundle.canonical_hash = original


def test_resolution_snapshot_rejects_wrong_prompt_a(db: Session) -> None:
    bundle, _prompt_a, sampling = _bundle(
        db,
        include_product_prompt=True,
    )
    wrong = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="Wrong A",
        version="wrong-A-v1",
        system_prompt="wrong",
        user_prompt="wrong",
        rubric_version="wrong",
        status="draft",
    )
    db.add(wrong)
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="StrategyBundle 不一致"):
        build_routed_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=wrong,
            sampling_policy=sampling,
            precheck=_precheck(),
            resolution_timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )


def test_v2_bundle_cannot_enter_v3_resolver(db: Session) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, space_b, _product_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=space_b,
        rubric_version=space_b.rubric_version,
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    assert bundle.strategy_schema_version == "strategy-bundle-v2"
    with pytest.raises(
        RoutedStrategyContractError,
        match="只有 strategy-bundle-v3",
    ):
        load_frozen_evaluation_profiles(bundle)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_capacity_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import (
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxRequest, enqueue_runtime_outbox
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    complete_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
    fail_runtime_step,
)
from tests.test_workflow_runtime_api import _client


def _create_run(db, version_id: int, suffix: str):
    return create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=version_id,
            idempotency_key=f"capacity-{suffix}",
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            queue_class="validation",
            runtime_context={"run_mode": "capacity_test"},
            input_manifest={"content_key": f"capacity:{suffix}"},
            owner="capacity-test",
        ),
        actor="admin",
    )


def test_runtime_capacity_is_derived_from_persisted_queue_facts() -> None:
    client, SessionForTest = _client("admin")
    current = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with SessionForTest() as db:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id))
        assert version is not None

        completed_run = _create_run(db, version.id, "completed")
        completed_attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
            db,
            "capacity-complete-worker",
            global_limit=10,
        )
        completed_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, completed_attempt_id)
        assert completed_attempt is not None and completed_attempt.lease_token
        complete_runtime_step(
            db,
            completed_attempt.id,
            completed_attempt.lease_token,
            {"done": True},
        )

        leased_run = _create_run(db, version.id, "leased")
        leased_attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
            db,
            "capacity-running-worker",
            global_limit=10,
        )
        assert leased_attempt_id is not None

        failed_run = _create_run(db, version.id, "dead-letter")
        failed_attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
            db,
            "capacity-failure-worker",
            global_limit=10,
        )
        failed_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, failed_attempt_id)
        assert failed_attempt is not None and failed_attempt.lease_token
        fail_runtime_step(
            db,
            failed_attempt.id,
            failed_attempt.lease_token,
            "capacity_contract_invalid",
            "字段合同不匹配",
            retryable=False,
        )

        queued_run = _create_run(db, version.id, "queued")

        completed_dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == completed_attempt.id
            )
        )
        queued_attempt = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == queued_run.id
            )
        )
        queued_dispatch = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == queued_attempt.id
            )
        )
        assert completed_dispatch is not None and queued_dispatch is not None
        completed_dispatch.updated_at = current - timedelta(seconds=60)
        queued_dispatch.available_at = current - timedelta(minutes=10)

        outbox = enqueue_runtime_outbox(
            db,
            RuntimeOutboxRequest(
                event_key="capacity-outbox-event",
                target_contract_key="logical.knowledge_graph.output",
                business_idempotency_key=(
                    f"knowledge_graph:asset:42:mechanism:{version.id}"
                ),
                mechanism_version_id=version.id,
                payload={"asset_id": 42, "field": "value"},
            ),
        )
        outbox.available_at = current - timedelta(minutes=20)
        db.commit()

        assert completed_run.status == "succeeded"
        assert leased_run.status == "running"
        assert failed_run.status == "failed"

    response = client.get("/api/runtime/capacity")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    payload = response.json()
    validation = next(
        item for item in payload["queues"] if item["queue_class"] == "validation"
    )
    assert validation["queued"] == 1
    assert validation["leased"] == 1
    assert validation["dead_letters"] == 1
    assert validation["oldest_queued_age_seconds"] >= 590
    assert validation["completed_last_5m"] == 1
    assert validation["completion_rate_per_second"] > 0
    assert payload["dead_letters"] == {
        "runtime": 1,
        "outbox": 0,
        "total": 1,
            "regression": 0,
    }
    assert payload["outbox"]["queued"] == 1
    assert payload["outbox"]["oldest_queued_age_seconds"] >= 1190
    assert payload["outbox"]["targets"][0]["target_contract_key"] == (
        "logical.knowledge_graph.output"
    )
    assert payload["backpressure"]["state"] == "paused"
    assert payload["backpressure"]["safe_queueing"] is True
    assert payload["backpressure"]["paused"] is True
    assert payload["backpressure"]["pause_reason"]
    assert payload["estimated_drain_seconds"] is not None
    assert payload["estimated_drain_seconds"] > 0
    assert payload["total_pending"] == 3
    assert db_count_is_one(SessionForTest, RuntimeOutboxEvent)


def db_count_is_one(SessionForTest, model) -> bool:
    with SessionForTest() as db:
        return len(db.scalars(select(model)).all()) == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_capacity_config.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app import config


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clear_settings_cache() -> None:
    config.get_settings.cache_clear()
    yield
    config.get_settings.cache_clear()


def _load(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path,
    **values: str,
):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATA_DIR", str(tmp_path / "data"))
    for name in (
        "DATABASE_URL",
        "DATABASE_URL_FILE",
        "WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT",
        "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT",
        "DATABASE_POOL_SIZE",
        "DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW",
    ):
        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)
    for name, value in values.items():
        monkeypatch.setenv(name, value)
    config.get_settings.cache_clear()
    return config.get_settings()


def test_runtime_capacity_defaults_are_safe(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    settings = _load(monkeypatch, tmp_path)

    assert settings.worker_process_count is None
    assert settings.worker_cluster_expected_count is None
    assert settings.database_pool_size == 2
    assert settings.database_max_overflow == 0


def test_runtime_capacity_accepts_24_workers(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    settings = _load(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT="24",
        DATABASE_POOL_SIZE="2",
        DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW="0",
    )

    assert settings.worker_process_count == 24
    assert settings.database_pool_size == 2
    assert settings.database_max_overflow == 0


def test_runtime_capacity_accepts_cluster_health_target(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    settings = _load(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT="1",
        WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT="193",
    )

    assert settings.worker_process_count == 1
    assert settings.worker_cluster_expected_count == 193


def test_database_url_can_be_loaded_from_secret_file(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    secret_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://app:secret@mysql/labels\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    settings = _load(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        DATABASE_URL_FILE=str(secret_file),
    )

    assert settings.database_url == "mysql+pymysql://app:secret@mysql/labels"


def test_database_url_environment_wins_over_secret_file(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    settings = _load(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        DATABASE_URL="mysql+pymysql://app:env-secret@mysql/labels",
        DATABASE_URL_FILE=str(tmp_path / "missing-secret"),
    )

    assert settings.database_url == (
        "mysql+pymysql://app:env-secret@mysql/labels"
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("file_state", ["missing", "empty"])
def test_database_url_secret_file_fails_closed(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    file_state: str,
) -> None:
    secret_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    if file_state == "empty":
        secret_file.write_text(" \n", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="DATABASE_URL_FILE"):
        _load(
            monkeypatch,
            tmp_path,
            DATABASE_URL_FILE=str(secret_file),
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("name", "value"),
    [
        ("WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT", "33"),
        ("WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT", "many"),
        ("WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT", "0"),
        ("WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT", "10001"),
        ("WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT", "many"),
        ("DATABASE_POOL_SIZE", "0"),
        ("DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW", "-1"),
    ],
)
def test_runtime_capacity_rejects_unsafe_values(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    name: str,
    value: str,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=name):
        _load(monkeypatch, tmp_path, **{name: value})

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_dead_letter_replay.py =====
"""A replayed dead letter must get its own model invocation identity.

A model call is identified by (step_attempt_id, invocation_key), and
fail_model_invocation refuses to re-settle an already failed invocation with a
different error. While replay_dead_letter reused the failed attempt, a replayed
model step could therefore never finish: the retry failed differently, the
ledger raised model_invocation_idempotency_conflict, the worker dropped the
step, the lease expired and it dead-lettered again - without ever reaching the
provider. Observed twice in production on 2026-09-15, including on a completely
idle system.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem
from app.runtime_queue import RuntimeQueueError, replay_dead_letter
from test_workflow_runtime import _engine

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 15, 4, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
SNAPSHOT_HASH = "16a0eeb0791b6c92451fd284dd9f599e0a7dbe7f6ebea6e2d2d06c7f74aec112"
INPUT_HASH = "c96c6d5be8d08a12e7b5cdc1b207fa6b2430974c86803d8891675e76fd992c20"
CHECKPOINT_HASH = "47320987f9a49d5b00119b960f247a956773f57543982b8bfcb6da5bb3afd9ef"


def _dead_lettered(db: Session, *, step_key: str = "prompt_b") -> RuntimeDispatchItem:
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key="replay-run-1",
        idempotency_key="replay-run-1",
        workflow_definition_id=1,
        workflow_version_id=1,
        snapshot_json="{}",
        snapshot_hash=SNAPSHOT_HASH,
        total_steps=13,
        completed_steps=6,
        requested_by=1,
        owner="ops",
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        source_type="content_ingress_event",
        source_id=1,
        status="failed",
        current_step_key=step_key,
        failed_steps=1,
        error_code="structured_output_schema_mismatch",
        error_message="模型输出不符合提示词节点冻结的 JSON Schema",
        finished_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=5),
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=run.id,
        step_key=step_key,
        step_type="model_call",
        sequence=6,
        script_version_id=1,
        status="failed",
        attempt_no=2,
        idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:{step_key}:{INPUT_HASH}:attempt:2",
        input_manifest_json='{"frozen": true}',
        input_hash=INPUT_HASH,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
        checkpoint_hash=CHECKPOINT_HASH,
        last_error_code="structured_output_schema_mismatch",
        last_error_message="模型输出不符合提示词节点冻结的 JSON Schema",
        finished_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=5),
    )
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    dispatch = RuntimeDispatchItem(
        step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
        queue_class="recovery",
        priority=100,
        status="dead_lettered",
        available_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=10),
        attempt_count=2,
        max_attempts=2,
        last_error_code="structured_output_schema_mismatch",
        last_error_message="模型输出不符合提示词节点冻结的 JSON Schema",
        dead_lettered_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=5),
    )
    db.add(dispatch)
    db.flush()
    return dispatch


def test_replay_moves_the_dispatch_onto_a_fresh_attempt(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            dispatch = _dead_lettered(db)
            original_attempt_id = dispatch.step_attempt_id
            db.commit()
            item_id = dispatch.id

        with Session(engine) as db:
            replayed = replay_dead_letter(
                db, item_id, actor="ops", reason="供应商输出抖动，人工重放", now=NOW
            )
            db.commit()
            assert replayed.status == "queued"
            # The identity that the model ledger keys on must have moved.
            assert replayed.step_attempt_id != original_attempt_id

        with Session(engine) as db:
            original = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, original_attempt_id)
            # The failed attempt and its evidence stay exactly as recorded.
            assert original.status == "failed"
            assert original.attempt_no == 2
            assert original.last_error_code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
            assert original.finished_at is not None

            dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, item_id)
            replacement = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, dispatch.step_attempt_id)
            assert replacement.attempt_no == 3
            assert replacement.status == "retryable"
            assert replacement.last_error_code == ""
            assert replacement.finished_at is None
            assert replacement.step_key == original.step_key
            assert replacement.input_hash == original.input_hash
            assert replacement.input_manifest_json == original.input_manifest_json
            assert replacement.idempotency_key.endswith("attempt:3")
            assert replacement.idempotency_key != original.idempotency_key

            run = db.get(ProductionRun, original.run_id)
            assert run.status == "retryable"
            assert run.error_code == ""
            assert run.failed_steps == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_replay_numbers_the_new_attempt_above_every_existing_one(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            dispatch = _dead_lettered(db)
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, dispatch.step_attempt_id)
            # A later attempt for the same step exists; reusing attempt_no + 1
            # would collide with it on the idempotency key.
            db.add(
                ProductionStepAttempt(
                    run_id=attempt.run_id,
                    step_key=attempt.step_key,
                    step_type=attempt.step_type,
                    sequence=attempt.sequence,
                    script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
                    status="failed",
                    attempt_no=5,
                    idempotency_key=f"replay-run-1:{attempt.step_key}:{INPUT_HASH}:attempt:5",
                    input_manifest_json=attempt.input_manifest_json,
                    input_hash=attempt.input_hash,
                    output_manifest_json="{}",
                    checkpoint_json="{}",
                    checkpoint_hash=CHECKPOINT_HASH,
                    last_error_code="STEP_LEASE_EXPIRED",
                    last_error_message="Worker 租约过期",
                )
            )
            db.commit()
            item_id = dispatch.id

        with Session(engine) as db:
            replay_dead_letter(db, item_id, actor="ops", reason="人工重放", now=NOW)
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, item_id)
            replacement = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, dispatch.step_attempt_id)
            assert replacement.attempt_no == 6
            numbers = sorted(
                row.attempt_no
                for row in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == replacement.run_id
                    )
                )
            )
            assert numbers == [2, 5, 6], numbers
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_replay_still_refuses_anything_that_is_not_a_current_dead_letter(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            dispatch = _dead_lettered(db)
            dispatch.status = "queued"
            db.commit()
            item_id = dispatch.id

        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeQueueError, match="dead_letter_replay_state_invalid"):
                replay_dead_letter(db, item_id, actor="ops", reason="不应通过", now=NOW)

        with Session(engine) as db:
            # No attempt may be created for a refused replay.
            assert db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no == 3
                )
            ) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["actor", "reason"])
def test_replay_still_requires_an_actor_and_a_reason(tmp_path, field) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            dispatch = _dead_lettered(db)
            db.commit()
            item_id = dispatch.id

        with Session(engine) as db:
            kwargs = {"actor": "ops", "reason": "人工重放"}
            kwargs[field] = "   "
            with pytest.raises(ValueError):
                replay_dead_letter(db, item_id, now=NOW, **kwargs)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_foundation_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import models
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import runner as migration_runner


def test_migration_82_upgrades_existing_runtime_dispatch_rows(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-v82.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE workflow_versions (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE production_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE production_step_attempts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO production_step_attempts(id) VALUES (7)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                """
                CREATE TABLE runtime_dispatch_items (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    step_attempt_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE
                        REFERENCES production_step_attempts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                    queue_class VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
                    priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 50,
                    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued'
                        CHECK(status IN ('queued','leased','completed','canceled')),
                    available_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
                """
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO runtime_dispatch_items"
                "(id, step_attempt_id, queue_class, priority, status) "
                "VALUES (9, 7, 'validation', 80, 'queued')"
            )

            migration_runner._migration_082_add_runtime_foundation(connection)

            inspector = inspect(connection)
            assert {"workflow_snapshot_blobs", "runtime_outbox_events"} <= set(
                inspector.get_table_names()
            )
            assert "snapshot_blob_id" in {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("production_runs")
            }
            dispatch_columns = {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("runtime_dispatch_items")
            }
            assert {
                "attempt_count",
                "max_attempts",
                "lease_owner",
                "lease_token",
                "leased_at",
                "heartbeat_at",
                "lease_expires_at",
                "last_error_code",
                "last_error_message",
                "dead_lettered_at",
            } <= dispatch_columns
            preserved = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT id, step_attempt_id, priority, status, attempt_count, "
                "max_attempts FROM runtime_dispatch_items"
            ).one()
            assert tuple(preserved) == (9, 7, 80, "queued", 0, 3)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).fetchall() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_82_records_the_new_schema_version(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-ledger.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration_runner.run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == max(
                migration.version for migration in migration_runner.MIGRATIONS
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 82"
            ).scalar_one() == "add_runtime_foundation"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_snapshot_hash_and_outbox_business_identity_are_unique(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-identity.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            blob_type = models.WorkflowSnapshotBlob
            db.add_all(
                [
                    blob_type(
                        snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
                        canonical_size_bytes=2,
                        compressed_size_bytes=22,
                        compression="gzip",
                        payload_bytes=b"first",
                    ),
                    blob_type(
                        snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
                        canonical_size_bytes=2,
                        compressed_size_bytes=23,
                        compression="gzip",
                        payload_bytes=b"second",
                    ),
                ]
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            blob = blob_type(
                snapshot_hash="b" * 64,
                canonical_size_bytes=2,
                compressed_size_bytes=22,
                compression="gzip",
                payload_bytes=b"payload",
            )
            db.add(blob)
            db.flush()
            definition = models.WorkflowDefinition(
                workflow_key="runtime-foundation-test",
                name="Runtime foundation",
                owner="test",
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(definition)
            db.flush()
            version = models.WorkflowVersion(
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                version="1",
                step_manifest_json="[]",
                edge_manifest_json="[]",
                input_schema_json="{}",
                output_schema_json="{}",
                canonical_hash="c" * 64,
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(version)
            db.flush()
            event_type = models.RuntimeOutboxEvent
            common = {
                "target_contract_key": "shadow-target",
                "business_idempotency_key": "asset:1:mechanism:1",
                "mechanism_version_id": version.id,
                "payload_blob_id": blob.id,
                "payload_hash": blob.snapshot_hash,
            }
            db.add_all(
                [
                    event_type(event_key="event-1", **common),
                    event_type(event_key="event-2", **common),
                ]
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            assert db.scalar(
                select(models.WorkflowSnapshotBlob.id).where(
                    models.WorkflowSnapshotBlob.snapshot_hash == "b" * 64
                )
            ) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_long_tail_mysql.py =====
"""Real multi-process claim proof; only the configured disposable test database."""
import multiprocessing
import json
import pytest
from datetime import timedelta

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, release_model_quota
from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step
from test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling import NOW, ready, hint, claim
from test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
from test_model_quota import _pool


def _claim_one(url):
    from app.models import QueueSchedulerState
    assert url.database.startswith('labelsystem_test_')
    engine = create_engine(url)
    event.listen(engine, "connect", lambda c, _: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(c))
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = claim(db)
            count = db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1).dispatch_count
            db.commit()
            return count, item.attempt_id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_processes_share_per_queue_continuation_phase(mysql_engine):
    from app.models import QueueSchedulerState
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        old, _ = ready(db, version, 'old-first-a', age=1800)
        old_id = old.id
        rules = {ready(db, version, f'old-rule-{i}', kind='projection', sequence=12, age=900)[0].id
                 for i in range(8)}
        db.commit()
    with multiprocessing.get_context('spawn').Pool(4) as workers:
        results = workers.map_async(_claim_one, [mysql_engine.url]*8).get(timeout=120)
    ordered = [identity for _, identity in sorted(results)]
    assert len(set(ordered)) == 8
    assert all(identity in rules for identity in ordered[:4])
    assert ordered[4] == old_id
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert json.loads(db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1).continuation_counts_json)['validation'] == 3


def _consume_local(url):
    if not url.database.startswith("labelsystem_test_"):
        raise ValueError("only disposable test schemas are allowed")
    engine = create_engine(url)
    event.listen(engine, "connect", lambda c, _: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(c))
    completed = []
    try:
        for _ in range(80):
            with Session(engine) as db:
                item = claim(db)
                if item is None:
                    db.rollback()
                    break
                complete_runtime_step(db, item.attempt_id, item.lease_token, {"ok": True}, now=NOW)
                db.commit()
                completed.append(item.attempt_id)
        return completed
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_thousand_waiters_and_four_processes_complete_ready_outputs(mysql_engine):
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="long-tail-mysql", concurrency=24)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        leases = [acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id,
            owner_key=f"already-waiting:{i}", now=NOW) for i in range(22)]
        release_token = leases[0].lease_token
        for i in range(1000):
            a, dispatch = ready(db, version, f"blocked-a-{i}", age=45)
            hint(a, dispatch, pool, headroom=2)
        local_ids = {ready(db, version, f"ready-output-{i}", kind="projection", sequence=12)[0].id
                     for i in range(50)}
        b, dispatch = ready(db, version, "ready-b", sequence=6)
        hint(b, dispatch, pool, headroom=2)
        db.add(ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=b.run_id, step_key="completed-a", step_type="model_call",
            sequence=2, script_version_id=b.script_version_id, status="succeeded", attempt_no=1,
            idempotency_key="completed-a", input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a"*64,
            output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}"))
        online, dispatch = ready(db, version, "online-fallback")
        hint(online, dispatch, pool, headroom=0)
        online_id, b_id = online.id, b.id
        db.commit()
    with multiprocessing.get_context("spawn").Pool(4) as workers:
        results = workers.map_async(_consume_local, [mysql_engine.url] * 4).get(timeout=120)
    ids = [i for result in results for i in result]
    assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)) == 51
    assert set(ids) == local_ids | {online_id}
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert claim(db) is None
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=release_token, now=NOW)
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == b_id
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
                         .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc()).limit(1)) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('wait_code', ['model_provider_resource_wait', 'model_batch_submission_wait'])
def test_mysql_provider_wait_is_hard_gate_even_when_pool_is_idle(mysql_engine, wait_code):
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key='provider-wait-idle', concurrency=24)
        waiters = set()
        for i in range(64):
            attempt, dispatch = ready(db, version, f'provider-wait-{i}')
            hint(attempt, dispatch, pool)
            dispatch.last_error_code = wait_code
            dispatch.available_at = NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)
            waiters.add(attempt.id)
        ready_ids = {ready(db, version, f'completed-output-{i}', kind='projection', sequence=12)[0].id
                     for i in range(8)}
        db.commit()
    with multiprocessing.get_context('spawn').Pool(4) as workers:
        results = workers.map_async(_consume_local, [mysql_engine.url]*4).get(timeout=120)
    completed = [item for result in results for item in result]
    assert len(completed) == len(set(completed)) == 8 and set(completed) == ready_ids
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert claim(db, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=119)) is None
        assert claim(db, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)).attempt_id in waiters
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling.py =====
"""Queue behavior with waiting provider calls; no network or fake provider results."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem
from app.runtime_queue import ClaimRequest, claim_runtime_item
from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run
from test_workflow_runtime import _engine, _seed_active_workflow
from test_model_quota import _pool
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, release_model_quota

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 10, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('sla_enabled', ['0', '1'])
def test_multiple_workflows_share_wait_slots_instead_of_old_backlog_monopolizing(tmp_path, monkeypatch, sla_enabled):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', sla_enabled)
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    _assert_workflow_fairness(engine)
    engine.dispose()


def _assert_workflow_fairness(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db:
        a = _seed_active_workflow(db, workflow_key='batch.large')
        b = _seed_active_workflow(db, workflow_key='batch.small')
        old = [ready(db, a, f'large-{i}', age=600)[0].id for i in range(10)]
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == old[0]
        newcomer, _ = ready(db, b, 'small-1', age=1)
        # One old workflow already has a long provider wait. A new category
        # gets the next same-priority slot, even with ten older FIFO items.
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == newcomer.id
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == old[1]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('sla_enabled', ['0', '1'])
def test_mysql_workflow_fairness_uses_current_shared_occupancy(mysql_engine, monkeypatch, sla_enabled):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', sla_enabled)
    from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    _assert_workflow_fairness(mysql_engine)


def ready(db, version, key, *, kind="model_call", sequence=0, age=30):
    run = create_production_run(db, ProductionRunRequest(
        workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key=key,
        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="validation",
        runtime_context={}, input_manifest={}, owner="platform"), actor="test")
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    # This is a queue-only fixture: no model executor sees the identity script.
    attempt.step_type, attempt.sequence = kind, sequence
    dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
    dispatch.available_at = NOW - timedelta(seconds=age)
    db.flush()
    return attempt, dispatch


def claim(db, now=NOW):
    return claim_runtime_item(db, ClaimRequest(worker_id="test", global_limit=2000, now=now))


def hint(attempt, dispatch, pool, *, headroom=0):
    attempt.status = "retryable"
    attempt.checkpoint_json = json.dumps({"keep_me": "correction", "_model_quota_wait": {
        "pool_id": pool.id, "headroom": headroom,
        "recheck_at": (NOW + timedelta(seconds=60)).timestamp()}})
    dispatch.last_error_code = "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"


def test_ready_rule_bypasses_old_prompt_backlog_but_fifo_gets_a_turn(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        first, _ = ready(db, version, "old-a", age=45)
        local_ids = {ready(db, version, f"rule-{i}", kind="rule_eval", sequence=3)[0].id for i in range(8)}
        for _ in range(4):
            assert claim(db).attempt_id in local_ids
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == first.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_b_continuation_precedes_new_a(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        first, _ = ready(db, version, "new-a", age=45)
        b, _ = ready(db, version, "b", sequence=6)
        prior = ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=b.run_id, step_key="completed-a", step_type="model_call",
            sequence=2, script_version_id=b.script_version_id, status="succeeded", attempt_no=1,
            idempotency_key="completed-a", input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a"*64,
            output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}")
        db.add(prior)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == b.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_old_b_continuation_still_precedes_old_a_backlog(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        ready(db, version, "old-a", age=1800)
        b, _ = ready(db, version, "old-b", sequence=6, age=900)
        db.add(ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=b.run_id, step_key="completed-a",
            step_type="model_call", sequence=2, script_version_id=b.script_version_id,
            status="succeeded", attempt_no=1, idempotency_key="prior-a",
            input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a"*64, output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}"))
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == b.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_pacing_wait_does_not_wake_just_because_concurrency_is_free(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="paced", concurrency=24)
        attempt, dispatch = ready(db, version, "paced")
        hint(attempt, dispatch, pool)
        dispatch.last_error_code = "model_batch_submission_wait"
        dispatch.available_at = NOW + timedelta(seconds=45)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db) is None
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=44)) is None
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=45)).attempt_id == attempt.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_saturated_pool_skips_waiters_and_wakes_on_release(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="long-tail", concurrency=1)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        lease = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="long-tail", now=NOW)
        blocked, dispatch = ready(db, version, "blocked", age=1000)
        hint(blocked, dispatch, pool)
        rule, _ = ready(db, version, "local", kind="rule_eval", sequence=3)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == rule.id
        assert claim(db) is None  # Not another image preparation / quota rejection.
        assert blocked.attempt_no == 1
        assert json.loads(blocked.checkpoint_json)["keep_me"] == "correction"
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=lease.lease_token, now=NOW)
        # Releasing capacity wakes without waiting for the retry timer.
        dispatch.available_at = NOW + timedelta(seconds=5)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == blocked.id
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cooldown,headroom", [(True, 0), (False, 1)])
def test_hint_respects_cooldown_and_online_headroom(tmp_path, cooldown, headroom):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="protected", concurrency=2)
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="one", now=NOW)
        if cooldown:
            pool.cooldown_until = NOW + timedelta(seconds=30)
        blocked, dispatch = ready(db, version, "blocked")
        hint(blocked, dispatch, pool, headroom=headroom)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db) is None
        # Stale hints must never permanently hide work after route/pool changes.
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=61)).attempt_id == blocked.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_saturation_stops_before_media_preparation_and_preserves_frozen_run(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="saturated-before-image", concurrency=1)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="existing-call")
        attempt.checkpoint_json = '{"operator_note":"keep"}'
        db.commit()
        run_id, attempt_id = run.id, attempt.id
        frozen = run.snapshot_json
        token, owner = attempt.lease_token, attempt.lease_owner

    def forbidden_preparation(*args, **kwargs):
        pytest.fail("A saturated model pool must not read/prepare images again")
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "prepare_prompt_invocation", forbidden_preparation)
    operation = executor._real_prompt_attempt_steps(
        attempt_id=attempt_id, node=node, run_snapshot=json.loads(frozen),
        input_manifest={}, lease_token=token, worker_id=owner, model_client_type=None)
    with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as error:
        next(operation)
    assert error.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).snapshot_json == frozen
        checkpoint = json.loads(db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).checkpoint_json)
        assert checkpoint["operator_note"] == "keep"
        assert checkpoint["_model_quota_wait"]["pool_id"] == pool.id
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []
    engine.dispose()


def test_thousand_waiters_do_not_delay_ready_output_or_spend_model_attempts(tmp_path):
    from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="thousand-long-tails", concurrency=24)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        leases = [acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                  owner_key=f"already-in-flight:{i}", now=NOW) for i in range(22)]
        for i in range(1000):
            attempt, dispatch = ready(db, version, f"backlog-{i}", age=1000)
            hint(attempt, dispatch, pool, headroom=2)
        local, _ = ready(db, version, "ready-output", kind="projection", sequence=12)
        online, online_dispatch = ready(db, version, "online-fallback")
        hint(online, online_dispatch, pool, headroom=0)
        db.flush()
        claimed = claim(db)
        assert claimed.attempt_id == local.id
        complete_runtime_step(db, local.id, claimed.lease_token, {"ok": True}, now=NOW)
        assert local.status == "succeeded"
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == online.id
        assert claim(db) is None
        # 1000 queued items never start preparation or burn a model retry.
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
                         .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc()).limit(1)) == 1
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=leases[0].lease_token, now=NOW)
        assert claim(db) is not None
    engine.dispose()


def test_preflight_does_not_block_authoritative_replay_of_own_reservation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="own-reservation", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.flush()
        reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node,
            run_snapshot=json.loads(run.snapshot_json), invocation_key="root")
        db.commit()
        preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
            lease_token=attempt.lease_token, worker_id=attempt.lease_owner)
        assert "_model_quota_wait" not in json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
    engine.dispose()


def test_online_quota_wait_does_not_reuse_expired_batch_deadline(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, _, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        limits = snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"][attempt.step_key]
        limits["online_fallback_limits"] = dict(limits)
        run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
        pool = _pool(db, key="online-full", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="existing")
        db.commit()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError):
            preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
                lease_token=attempt.lease_token, worker_id=attempt.lease_owner,
                transport_mode="online", transport_deadline=now-timedelta(seconds=1))
        wait = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)["_model_quota_wait"]
        assert wait["recheck_at"] >= (now+timedelta(seconds=30)).timestamp()
    engine.dispose()


def test_postgres_text_checkpoint_is_cast_before_json_operators():
    from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql
    from app.runtime_admission import ready_dispatch_predicate
    sql = str(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
        ready_dispatch_predicate(NOW, dialect="postgresql")).compile(dialect=postgresql.dialect()))
    assert "CAST(production_step_attempts.checkpoint_json AS JSON)" in sql
    assert "production_step_attempts.checkpoint_json ->" not in sql


def test_prior_attempt_same_key_does_not_bypass_preflight(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, old, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="prior-attempt", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.flush()
        reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=old.id, node=node,
            run_snapshot=json.loads(run.snapshot_json), invocation_key="root")
        attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=old.run_id, step_key=old.step_key, step_type=old.step_type,
            sequence=old.sequence, script_version_id=old.script_version_id, status="running", attempt_no=2,
            idempotency_key="new-attempt", input_manifest_json=old.input_manifest_json, input_hash=old.input_hash,
            output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}", lease_token=old.lease_token, lease_owner=old.lease_owner)
        db.add(attempt)
        db.commit()
